 }

diff --git a/apps/api/scripts/lib/kael-playbook-source-hash.mjs b/apps/api/scripts/lib/kael-playbook-source-hash.mjs

index 00000000..155694d5

+import { createHash } from 'node:crypto'
+
+export function sha256Hex(value) {
+  return createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex')
+}
+
+// One path once, in a fixed order, so the recorded list and the hashed list are the same list and reordering the
+// source of the list cannot change a report's hash.
+export function normalizeSourceFiles(files) {
+  return [...new Set(files)].sort()
+}
+
+// sha256_path_and_file_sha256_v1: every path with the hash of its text, in path order. A changed byte, a renamed
+// file, a removed file and an added file each change the result.
+export function computeSourceTreeHash(files, readText) {
+  const parts = normalizeSourceFiles(files).map((path) => `${path}\n${sha256Hex(readText(path))}`)
+  return sha256Hex(parts.join('\n'))
+}

+// Scoring for a blind emergency evaluation set that someone other than the implementer wrote and sealed. It reads a set,
+// classifies each row with the classifier it is given, and returns counts, intervals and a verdict. It never returns or
+// logs a row's text, so a report can be attached to a review without leaking the set.
+
+import { createHash } from 'node:crypto'
+
+export const BLIND_ROUTES = ['fire', 'medical', 'police', 'electrical_water', 'gas', 'uncertain'] as const
+export type BlindRoute = (typeof BLIND_ROUTES)[number]
+
+export type BlindRow = {
+  id: string
+  text: string
+  expected: BlindRoute | null
+  language: 'vi' | 'en'
+  kind: 'clear' | 'judgement'
+  service?: string
+}
+
+export type BlindSet = {
+  schema: 'kael_emergency_blind_set.v1'
+  reviewer_attestation: {
+    reviewer: string
+    author_is_implementer: boolean
+    sealed_at: string
+    sealed_sha256: string
+    source: string
+  }
+  rows: BlindRow[]
+}
+
+// Proposed release thresholds. They are a recommendation for the owner and have no authority until the owner approves
+// them, which the report states.
+export const PROPOSED_BLIND_THRESHOLDS = {
+  overall_recall_lower_bound: 0.9,
+  per_route_recall_lower_bound: 0.8,
+  false_positive_upper_bound: 0.05,
+  min_positives_per_route: 30,
+  min_negatives: 100,
+  owner_approved: false,
+} as const
+
+export function canonicalBlindHash(rows: readonly BlindRow[]): string {
+  return createHash('sha256')
+    .update(JSON.stringify(rows.map((row) => [row.id, row.text, row.expected, row.language, row.kind, row.service ?? null])))
+    .digest('hex')
+}
+
+// Wilson score interval; null when there is nothing to divide.
+export function wilsonInterval(successes: number, n: number, z = 1.96): { low: number; high: number } | null {
+  if (n <= 0) return null
+  const p = successes / n
+  const denominator = 1 + (z * z) / n
+  const centre = p + (z * z) / (2 * n)
+  const margin = z * Math.sqrt((p * (1 - p) + (z * z) / (4 * n)) / n)
+  return { low: Math.max(0, (centre - margin) / denominator), high: Math.min(1, (centre + margin) / denominator) }
+}
+
+// The smallest sample in which zero misses still proves a recall of at least `lowerBound` at the same confidence.
+export function zeroMissSampleFor(lowerBound: number, z = 1.96): number {
+  for (let n = 1; n < 100_000; n += 1) {
+    const interval = wilsonInterval(n, n, z)
+    if (interval && interval.low >= lowerBound) return n
+  }
+  return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
+}
+
+export function validateBlindSet(set: unknown): string[] {
+  const errors: string[] = []
+  const value = set as Partial<BlindSet> | null
+  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return ['the set is not an object']
+  if (value.schema !== 'kael_emergency_blind_set.v1') errors.push('schema must be kael_emergency_blind_set.v1')
+  const attestation = value.reviewer_attestation
+  if (!attestation) errors.push('reviewer_attestation is required')
+  else {
+    for (const key of ['reviewer', 'sealed_at', 'sealed_sha256', 'source'] as const) {
+      if (typeof attestation[key] !== 'string' || attestation[key].length === 0) errors.push(`reviewer_attestation.${key} is required`)
+    }
+    if (typeof attestation.author_is_implementer !== 'boolean') errors.push('reviewer_attestation.author_is_implementer must be a boolean')
+  }
+  if (!Array.isArray(value.rows) || value.rows.length === 0) return [...errors, 'rows must be a non-empty array']
+  const seen = new Set<string>()
+  for (const row of value.rows as Partial<BlindRow>[]) {
+    if (typeof row.id !== 'string' || row.id.length === 0) errors.push('every row needs an id')
+    else if (seen.has(row.id)) errors.push(`duplicate row id ${row.id}`)
+    else seen.add(row.id)
+    if (typeof row.text !== 'string' || row.text.trim().length === 0) errors.push(`row ${row.id} needs text`)
+    if (row.expected !== null && !BLIND_ROUTES.includes(row.expected as BlindRoute)) errors.push(`row ${row.id} has an unknown expected route`)
+    if (row.language !== 'vi' && row.language !== 'en') errors.push(`row ${row.id} needs a language of vi or en`)
+    if (row.kind !== 'clear' && row.kind !== 'judgement') errors.push(`row ${row.id} needs a kind of clear or judgement`)
+  }
+  return errors
+}
+
+type Ratio = { numerator: number; denominator: number; value: number | null; wilson95: { low: number; high: number } | null }
+const ratio = (numerator: number, denominator: number): Ratio => ({
+  numerator,
+  denominator,
+  value: denominator > 0 ? numerator / denominator : null,
+  wilson95: wilsonInterval(numerator, denominator),
+})
+
+export type BlindReport = ReturnType<typeof evaluateBlindSet>
+
+export function evaluateBlindSet(
+  set: BlindSet,
+  classify: (row: BlindRow) => BlindRoute | null,
+  options: { registeredSha256?: string | null } = {},
+) {
+  const problems = validateBlindSet(set)
+  if (problems.length > 0) throw new Error(`invalid blind set: ${problems.join('; ')}`)
+  const recomputed = canonicalBlindHash(set.rows)
+  const reasonsNotIndependent: string[] = []
+  if (set.reviewer_attestation.author_is_implementer !== false) reasonsNotIndependent.push('the reviewer did not attest that the author is not the implementer')
+  if (recomputed !== set.reviewer_attestation.sealed_sha256) reasonsNotIndependent.push('the rows do not match the sealed hash in the attestation')
+  if (!options.registeredSha256) reasonsNotIndependent.push('no hash was registered by the reviewer out of band')
+  else if (options.registeredSha256 !== recomputed) reasonsNotIndependent.push('the rows do not match the hash the reviewer registered out of band')
+
+  const clear = set.rows.filter((row) => row.kind === 'clear')
+  const results = clear.map((row) => ({ row, got: classify(row) }))
+  const positives = results.filter((entry) => entry.row.expected !== null)
+  const negatives = results.filter((entry) => entry.row.expected === null)
+  const detected = positives.filter((entry) => entry.got !== null)
+  const routed = positives.filter((entry) => entry.got === entry.row.expected)
+  const falseAlarms = negatives.filter((entry) => entry.got !== null)
+
+  const perRoute = Object.fromEntries(BLIND_ROUTES.map((route) => {
+    const rows = positives.filter((entry) => entry.row.expected === route)
+    return [route, {
+      detected: ratio(rows.filter((entry) => entry.got !== null).length, rows.length),
+      route_exact: ratio(rows.filter((entry) => entry.got === route).length, rows.length),
+    }]
+  })) as Record<BlindRoute, { detected: Ratio; route_exact: Ratio }>
+
+  const overall = ratio(detected.length, positives.length)
+  const falsePositive = ratio(falseAlarms.length, negatives.length)
+  const thresholds = PROPOSED_BLIND_THRESHOLDS
+  const shortfalls: string[] = []
+  for (const route of BLIND_ROUTES) {
+    if (perRoute[route].detected.denominator < thresholds.min_positives_per_route) shortfalls.push(`${route}: ${perRoute[route].detected.denominator} positives, ${thresholds.min_positives_per_route} needed`)
+  }
+  if (negatives.length < thresholds.min_negatives) shortfalls.push(`${negatives.length} negatives, ${thresholds.min_negatives} needed`)
+  const failing: string[] = []
+  if (routed.length < detected.length) failing.push('wrong emergency routes in clear positives')
+  if (overall.wilson95 && overall.wilson95.low < thresholds.overall_recall_lower_bound) failing.push('overall recall lower bound below the proposed threshold')
+  for (const route of BLIND_ROUTES) {
+    const interval = perRoute[route].detected.wilson95
+    if (interval && interval.low < thresholds.per_route_recall_lower_bound) failing.push(`${route} recall lower bound below the proposed threshold`)
+  }
+  if (falsePositive.wilson95 && falsePositive.wilson95.high > thresholds.false_positive_upper_bound) failing.push('false-positive upper bound above the proposed threshold')
+
+  return {
+    schema: 'kael_emergency_blind_report.v1' as const,
+    label: reasonsNotIndependent.length === 0 ? ('INDEPENDENT_BLIND' as const) : ('NOT_INDEPENDENT' as const),
+    independent_validation: reasonsNotIndependent.length === 0,
+    reasons_not_independent: reasonsNotIndependent,
+    evidence_tier: 'T0' as const,
+    sealed_sha256: recomputed,
+    sample: { rows: set.rows.length, clear_rows: clear.length, positives: positives.length, negatives: negatives.length, judgement_rows: set.rows.length - clear.length },
+    unseen_wording_recall: { detected: overall, route_exact: ratio(routed.length, positives.length), per_route: perRoute },
+    false_positives: falsePositive,
+    misses: positives.filter((entry) => entry.got === null).map((entry) => ({ id: entry.row.id, expected: entry.row.expected })),
+    wrong_routes: positives.filter((entry) => entry.got !== null && entry.got !== entry.row.expected).map((entry) => ({ id: entry.row.id, expected: entry.row.expected, got: entry.got })),
+    false_alarms: falseAlarms.map((entry) => ({ id: entry.row.id, got: entry.got })),
+    proposed_thresholds: thresholds,
+    sample_shortfalls: shortfalls,
+    below_proposed_thresholds: failing,
+    release_blocker: reasonsNotIndependent.length > 0 || shortfalls.length > 0 || failing.length > 0,
+    limits: [
+      'T0 rule classifier only: no model, no device, no real customer text.',
+      'The thresholds are a proposal and have no authority until the owner approves them.',
+      'A passing independent result supports a recall claim for the wording the reviewer sampled, not for wording nobody has seen.',
+    ],
+  }
+}

+const clone = <T,>(value: T): T => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as T

+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} session=${world.session().status}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(403)
+    expect(caseState(world), `${why} (the customer's confirmation is not the admin's to give or withdraw)`).toEqual(before)
+    expect((world.session().safe_metadata as Record<string, any>).intake_confirmation.status, why).toBe('pending')
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('refuses to submit or skip evidence for the customer, so no customer turn is forged', async () => {
+    const world = activeWorld()
+    const before = caseState(world)
+
+    const result = await world.request('/evidence', { language: 'vi', decision: 'skipped', skip_reason: 'Không có ảnh' }, { as: ADMIN })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(403)
+    expect(caseState(world), why).toEqual(before)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('still lets the owning customer make the same decisions, so the guard is the role and not the route', async () => {
+    const pending = pendingWorld('hvac')
+    const confirmed = await pending.request('/intake-confirmation', { decision: 'confirmed' }, { as: OWNER })
+    expect(confirmed.status, pillarWhy(PILLAR, JSON.stringify(confirmed.body).slice(0, 120))).toBe(200)
+    expect((pending.session().safe_metadata as Record<string, any>).intake_confirmation.status, pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'the owner confirmed')).toBe('confirmed')

+    expect(skipped.status, pillarWhy(PILLAR, JSON.stringify(skipped.body).slice(0, 120))).toBe(200)
+  })
+})

+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: a Case stream refuses the wrong role before anything is written`, () => {

+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: a request without its approval evidence fails before any call`, () => {

+    expect([400, 409], why).toContain(result.status)
+    expect(businessCalls().map((entry) => entry.table), `${why} (no database call before the approval evidence is valid)`).toEqual([])
+    expect(touched(), why).toEqual([])
+    expect(providerCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+})

+// The functions that record an approval or move a commitment, and the one module that defines each. The scan is by
+// definition, so a renamed wrapper still has to be listed here before a Kael-layer or emergency module may reach it.
+const APPROVAL_FUNCTIONS = {
+  confirmKaelChat: 'domains/kael-chat/confirm.service.ts',
+  requestDurableKaelConfirmation: 'domains/kael-chat/confirmation-operation.ts',
+  recoverLegacyKaelConfirmation: 'domains/kael-chat/legacy-confirmation-recovery.ts',
+  decideKaelIntakeConfirmation: 'domains/kael-chat/intake-confirmation.service.ts',
+  confirmSearch: 'domains/matching/flow.ts',
+  setJobMatchingPreference: 'domains/matching/matching-preference-selection.ts',
+  confirmWorkerCandidate: 'domains/matching/candidate.ts',
+  rejectWorkerCandidate: 'domains/matching/candidate.ts',
+  decideScopeChange: 'domains/job/scope-change/decision.ts',
+  decideRfqPrice: 'domains/job/rfq-price.ts',
+  authorizeApartmentAccess: 'domains/worker/apartment-access.ts',
+  createManualBankPaymentOrder: 'domains/payment/manual-bank.ts',
+  claimManualBankPayment: 'domains/payment/manual-bank.ts',
+  cancelJob: 'domains/customer/cancellation.ts',
+  requestCustomerCancellation: 'domains/customer/cancellation.ts',
+} as const
+const DEFINERS: readonly string[] = [...new Set(Object.values(APPROVAL_FUNCTIONS))]
+
+const SHARED_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '../../../../../../supabase/functions/mobile-api/_shared')
+const sourceFiles = (dir: string): string[] => readdirSync(dir).flatMap((entry) => {
+  const full = join(dir, entry)
+  if (statSync(full).isDirectory()) return sourceFiles(full)
+  return full.endsWith('.ts') && !full.endsWith('.test.ts') ? [full] : []
+})
+
+function directImports(file: string): string[] {
+  const text = readFileSync(file, 'utf8')
+  const found = [...text.matchAll(/(?:from|import)\s*\(?\s*["'](\.{1,2}\/[^"']+)["']/g)].map((match) => match[1] as string)
+  return found.map((specifier) => resolve(dirname(file), specifier)).filter((path) => path.endsWith('.ts'))
+}
+
+function closure(entry: string): Set<string> {
+  const seen = new Set<string>()
+  const queue = [resolve(entry)]
+  while (queue.length > 0) {
+    const file = queue.pop() as string
+    if (seen.has(file)) continue
+    seen.add(file)
+    for (const next of directImports(file)) queue.push(next)
+  }
+  return seen
+}
+
+const relativeToShared = (file: string) => relative(SHARED_ROOT, file).replace(/\\/g, '/')
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: nothing that answers an emergency or runs the model can record an approval`, () => {
+  const defines = (file: string, name: string) => new RegExp(`export (?:async )?function ${name}\\b`).test(readFileSync(join(SHARED_ROOT, file), 'utf8'))
+
+  it('lists, for every approval function, the module that still defines it', () => {
+    const stale = Object.entries(APPROVAL_FUNCTIONS).filter(([name, file]) => !defines(file, name)).map(([name, file]) => `${name} -> ${file}`)
+    expect(stale, pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'a moved or renamed function would leave the reachability rows below guarding nothing')).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('has no file of the Kael layer import from the domains or the HTTP layer', () => {
+    const offenders = sourceFiles(join(SHARED_ROOT, 'kael')).flatMap((file) =>
+      directImports(file)
+        .filter((target) => /[\\/]_shared[\\/](domains|http)[\\/]/.test(target))
+        .map((target) => `${relativeToShared(file)} -> ${relativeToShared(target)}`))
+    expect(offenders, pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'layers run http -> domains -> kael -> platform, never upward')).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('reaches no module that defines an approval function from anywhere in the Kael layer', () => {
+    const offenders = sourceFiles(join(SHARED_ROOT, 'kael')).flatMap((file) =>
+      [...closure(file)].map(relativeToShared).filter((reached) => DEFINERS.includes(reached)).map((reached) => `${relativeToShared(file)} -> ${reached}`))
+    expect(offenders, pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'the Kael layer proposes; it never reaches a module that records an approval')).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it.each([
+    'domains/kael-chat/emergency-turn.ts',
+    'domains/customer/assistant.ts',
+  ])('%s reaches no module that defines an approval function', (entry) => {
+    const reached = [...closure(join(SHARED_ROOT, entry))].map(relativeToShared)
+    const offenders = reached.filter((file) => DEFINERS.includes(file))
+    expect(offenders, pillarWhy(PILLAR, `${reached.length} files reached from ${entry}; an answer to a customer is a reply, never an approval`)).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('reaches a cancellation only from archiving a conversation, which is the customer deleting it', () => {
+    const callers = sourceFiles(SHARED_ROOT)
+      .filter((file) => /\bcloseLinkedCustomerJob\(/.test(readFileSync(file, 'utf8')))
+      .map(relativeToShared)
+      .sort()
+    expect(callers, pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'closeLinkedCustomerJob is defined in the turn module and called from the archive route only')).toEqual([
+      'domains/customer/kael-conversation-turn.ts',
+      'domains/customer/kael-conversation.ts',
+    ])
+    const turn = readFileSync(join(SHARED_ROOT, 'domains/customer/kael-conversation-turn.ts'), 'utf8')
+    const sendTurn = turn.slice(turn.indexOf('export async function sendCustomerKaelConversationTurn'), turn.indexOf('function customerReasoningCompletion'))
+    expect(/\b(cancelJob|requestCustomerCancellation|closeLinkedCustomerJob)\b/.test(sendTurn), pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'answering a normal-chat message never cancels a job')).toBe(false)
+  })
+})

+const clone = <T,>(value: T): T => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as T

+    serviceType: 'hvac',
+    parked: true,
+  } as never)?.guidance.route ?? null
+
+  it('routes two fields exactly as the same two sentences in one text, in either order', () => {
+    const disagreements: string[] = []
+    for (const first of POSITIVES) {
+      for (const second of POSITIVES) {
+        const apart = routeOf([first, second])
+        const together = routeOf([`${first}. ${second}`])
+        const swapped = routeOf([second, first])
+        if (apart !== together || apart !== swapped) disagreements.push(`${first} + ${second}: apart=${apart} together=${together} swapped=${swapped}`)
+      }
+    }
+    expect(disagreements, pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'a field order or a field boundary must never change which guidance a customer gets')).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('lets a later field report a danger that an earlier field only hints at', () => {
+    expect(routeOf(['Cần hỗ trợ khẩn cấp', 'Mình ngửi thấy mùi gas ở bếp']), pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'generic urgency in one field is not an answer on its own, but it must not hide a gas leak in the next')).toBe('gas')
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: the flagged safety path and the gate read the same text`, () => {
+  const flagged = { KAEL_PLAYBOOK_ELECTRICAL_ENABLED: 'true' }

+  it.each([{}, flagged])('answers a smoke report that only the transcript carries (env %j)', async (env) => {

+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} purposes=${result.purposes.join(',')}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)

+  })
+})

+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: evidence and skip reasons on the evidence route`, () => {
+  async function submit(world: CaseWorld, body: Record<string, unknown>, actor: Actor = OWNER) {
+    return world.request('/evidence', { language: 'vi', ...body }, { as: actor })

+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)

+    const world = evidenceWorld()

+

+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} body=${JSON.stringify(result.body).slice(0, 140)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(routesOf(world), why).toEqual(['gas'])
+    expect(clone({ scope: world.session().diagnosis_scope, status: world.session().status, phase: world.session().case_phase }), `${why} (the reply does not clear the request for evidence or move the phase)`).toEqual(before)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+  })

+    const world = evidenceWorld()
+
+    const result = await submit(world, { decision: 'confirmed', message: 'Ổ cắm tóe lửa khi cắm phích', evidence_items: [note('Mình ngửi thấy mùi gas ở bếp')] })
+    expect(result.status, pillarWhy(PILLAR, JSON.stringify(result.body).slice(0, 120))).toBe(200)
+    expect(routesOf(world), pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'the gas guidance covers the wiring hazard too')).toEqual(['gas'])
+  })
+})

+function createWorld(service: Service) {
+  const world = pendingWorld(service)
+  world.db.tables.kael_chat_sessions.length = 0
+  world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns.length = 0
+  const inert: unknown = new Proxy(function () {}, {
+    get: (_target, property) => property === 'then'
+      ? (resolve: (value: unknown) => unknown) => Promise.resolve({ data: { id: 'conversation-stub' }, error: null }).then(resolve)
+      : () => inert,
+    apply: () => inert,
+  })
+  const client = world.db.client as unknown as { from: (table: string) => unknown }
+  const original = client.from.bind(client)
+  const sessionDefaults = {
+    job_id: null, status: 'active', case_phase: 'analysis', total_turns: 0, total_cost_usd: 0,
+    diagnosis_scope: null, started_at: '2026-10-20T00:00:00.000Z', created_at: '2026-10-20T00:00:00.000Z',
+    estimate_ready_at: null, scheduled_at: null, safe_metadata: {},
+  }
+  client.from = (table: string) => {
+    if (table === 'kael_customer_conversations') return inert
+    const query = original(table) as { insert: (value: Record<string, unknown>) => unknown }
+    if (table !== 'kael_chat_sessions') return query
+    const insert = query.insert.bind(query)
+    query.insert = (value) => insert({ ...sessionDefaults, ...value })
+    return query
+  }
+  return world
+}
+
+function bookingBody(service: Service, overrides: Record<string, unknown>) {
+  const date = new Date(Date.now() + 5 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10)
+  const scheduledAt = new Date(Date.UTC(Number(date.slice(0, 4)), Number(date.slice(5, 7)) - 1, Number(date.slice(8, 10)), 9 - 7, 0)).toISOString()
+  return {
+    service_type: service,
+    profile_id: getKaelPerformanceProfile(service)!.id,
+    intake_source: 'booking',
+    intake_description: 'Cần thợ xem giúp thiết bị trong căn hộ vào cuối tuần',
+    message: 'Cần thợ xem giúp thiết bị trong căn hộ vào cuối tuần',
+    problem_chips: [PROBLEM_CHIP[service]],
+    address_label: 'Căn hộ thử nghiệm',
+    address_district: 'Quận 7',
+    scheduled_at: scheduledAt,
+    schedule_window: { date, start: '09:00', end: '11:00', time_zone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' },
+    client_request_id: '40800000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',
+    language: 'vi',
+    ...overrides,
+  }
+}

+async function createCase(world: CaseWorld, body: Record<string, unknown>) {
+  const handler = createMobileApiHandler({
+    authenticate: async () => ({
+      success: true as const,
+      user: { id: OWNER.id },
+      role: OWNER.role,
+      supabase: world.db.client,
+      privilegedSupabase: world.db.client,
+      userSupabase: world.db.client,
+    }) as never,
+    services: world.services,
+  })
+  const response = await handler(new Request('https://edge.test/mobile-api/kael/chat', {
+    method: 'POST',
+    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+    body: JSON.stringify(body),
+  }))
+  return { status: response.status, body: await response.json() as Record<string, any> }
+}

+    const viaShared = (path: string) => /\bcustomerAuthoredTexts\(/.test(readFileSync(path, 'utf8')) || /\bresolveCaseEmergency\(\{\s*message: confirmation\.intake\.description/.test(readFileSync(path, 'utf8'))
+    const unread = callers.filter((path) => !viaShared(path)).map((path) => relative(root, path))
+    expect(callers.length, pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'the scan found the known callers')).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4)

+  })

+export const PILLAR = {
+  id: 'P411-kael-case-emergency-closure-paths',
+  invariant:
+    'a reported emergency is never lost to a path that ends the request before the gate reads it: a create on an existing session with no typed message, an invalid schedule, a closed Case, or a write that fails. After authentication and ownership, such a request is answered with deterministic guidance through a response the contract already allows (the existing chat, or the same error code and status with the guidance in its message), writes nothing a closed or invalid request could not write, never confirms or moves the Case, asks no model, and an ordinary request is refused or ignored exactly as before; a classified emergency whose write fails leaves a redacted failure trace and still carries the guidance',
+  authority: [
+    'governance/RULES.md #7 (an emergency classification is not a workflow gate and never confirms, creates or advances money-impacting state)',
+    'governance/RULES.md #8 (a refusal or a failed write is visible, never a fabricated success)',
+    'governance/RULES.md #9 (logs carry ids, codes and safe metadata only, never raw chat text or PII)',
+    'KAWA_01_Foundation_Safety_Evaluation.pdf W01 (Case intake emergency coverage) and W03 (observability)',
+  ],
+  target: 'supabase/functions/mobile-api/_shared/domains/kael-chat/emergency-turn.ts',
+  layer: 'integration',
+  siblings: ['P408-kael-case-emergency-all-fields', 'P409-kael-case-emergency-write-integrity', 'P407-kael-case-emergency-pending-confirmation'],
+  mutation:
+    'answer only the typed message on a create with a session id, check the schedule or the closed status before the gate, drop the guidance from a refusal, write to a closed Case, or skip the failure trace: the voice-only, schedule, closed-Case and failure rows turn red',
+} as const satisfies PillarManifest
+
+installEdgeRuntimeTestHooks()
+
+const clone = <T,>(value: T): T => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as T
+const voice = (transcript: string) => ({ kind: 'voice_transcript', transcript, model_eligible: true })
+const BENIGN = 'Mình cần thợ xem giúp thiết bị trong căn hộ'
+const GAS = 'Mình ngửi thấy mùi gas ở bếp'
+
+const GENUINE: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, string, string]> = [
+  [GAS, 'gas', '114'],
+  ['Ổ cắm đang bốc khói', 'fire', '114'],
+  ['Chồng mình bị điện giật, nằm im không cử động', 'medical', '115'],
+  ['Có người đột nhập vào nhà', 'police', '113'],
+]
+
+const PAST = {
+  scheduled_at: '2020-01-01T02:00:00.000Z',
+  schedule_window: { date: '2020-01-01', start: '09:00', end: '11:00', time_zone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' },
+}
+const CLOSED = ['confirmed', 'abandoned', 'unsupported'] as const
+
+const snapshot = (world: CaseWorld) => clone({ session: world.session(), turns: world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns })
+const routesOf = (world: CaseWorld) =>
+  world.kaelTurns().map((turn) => (turn.safe_metadata as Record<string, unknown>).emergency_route).filter((route) => route !== undefined)
+
+async function createOn(world: CaseWorld, body: Record<string, unknown>, actor: Actor = OWNER) {
+  const handler = createMobileApiHandler({
+    authenticate: async () => ({
+      success: true as const,
+      user: { id: actor.id },
+      role: actor.role,
+      supabase: world.db.client,
+      privilegedSupabase: world.db.client,
+      userSupabase: world.db.client,
+    }) as never,
+    services: world.services,
+  })
+  const response = await handler(new Request('https://edge.test/mobile-api/kael/chat', {
+    method: 'POST',
+    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+    body: JSON.stringify({ session_id: CASE_SESSION_ID, service_type: 'hvac', language: 'vi', ...body }),
+  }))
+  return { status: response.status, body: await response.json() as Record<string, any> }
+}
+
+const failureText = (result: { body: Record<string, any> }) => JSON.stringify(result.body)
+const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
+const IDENTITY = {
+  releaseId: 'harness-0123456789ab-fedcba987654',
+  deploymentId: 'iwevizmsedyqozxlawwl_5e1f2a3b-4c5d-4e6f-8a9b-0c1d2e3f4a5b_297',
+  clientPlatform: 'ios',
+  clientBuildNumber: 57,
+  clientEasBuildId: '7d3c1f0e-8b2a-4c6d-9e5f-1a2b3c4d5e6f',
+  clientGitSha: 'a06afacef09420e5678fa928db1b23b088b954e4',
+  clientReleaseId: 'harness-0123456789ab-fedcba987654',
+} as const
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  __resetRateLimitStoreForTests()
+})

+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: a create on an existing session with no typed message`, () => {
+  it.each(GENUINE)('answers "%s" (%s) sent as a voice transcript alone', async (transcript, route, number) => {
+    const world = activeWorld()
+    const before = clone({ status: world.session().status, phase: world.session().case_phase, scope: world.session().diagnosis_scope })
+    const turnsBefore = world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns.length
+
+    const result = await createOn(world, { evidence_items: [voice(transcript)] })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} turns=${world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns.length}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(201)
+    expect(routesOf(world), why).toEqual([route])
+    expect(String(world.kaelTurns().at(-1)?.text_content), why).toContain(number)
+    expect(world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns.slice(turnsBefore).map((turn) => turn.role), `${why} (what the customer said, then one reply)`).toEqual(['customer', 'kael'])
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+    expect(clone({ status: world.session().status, phase: world.session().case_phase, scope: world.session().diagnosis_scope }), `${why} (the Case is where it was)`).toEqual(before)
+    expect(world.session().job_id ?? null, why).toBeNull()
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('answers a danger written only in the booking description, with no message and no evidence', async () => {
+    const world = activeWorld()
+
+    const result = await createOn(world, { intake_description: 'Bếp nhà mình có mùi gas rất nồng từ sáng' })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} body=${failureText(result).slice(0, 120)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(201)
+    expect(routesOf(world), why).toEqual(['gas'])
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('leaves an ordinary transcript exactly as it was: the chat is returned and nothing is written', async () => {
+    const world = activeWorld()
+    const before = snapshot(world)
+
+    const result = await createOn(world, { evidence_items: [voice('Máy treo tường, hai bộ')] })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(201)
+    expect(snapshot(world), why).toEqual(before)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('answers it while the booking confirmation is pending, and the confirmation stays pending', async () => {
+    const world = pendingWorld('hvac')

+    expect(routesOf(world), why).toEqual(['gas'])

+    expect(world.session().job_id ?? null, why).toBeNull()
+  })
+
+  it('answers it even when the request also carries an invalid schedule, and applies no schedule', async () => {
+    const world = activeWorld()
+
+    const result = await createOn(world, { evidence_items: [voice(GAS)], ...PAST })

+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(201)
+    expect(routesOf(world), why).toEqual(['gas'])
+    expect(world.session().scheduled_at ?? null, `${why} (the invalid schedule is not applied)`).toBeNull()
+  })
+
+  it('keeps refusing an invalid schedule on an ordinary voice-only request', async () => {
+    const world = activeWorld()

+    expect(failureText(result), why).toContain(HCMC_SCHEDULE_VALIDATION_MESSAGE)
+    expect(snapshot(world), why).toEqual(before)
+  })
+
+  it.each(CLOSED)('refuses a %s Case with the guidance in the same 409, and writes nothing', async (status) => {
+    const world = activeWorld('hvac', { status })
+    const before = snapshot(world)
+
+    const result = await createOn(world, { evidence_items: [voice(GAS)] })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} body=${failureText(result).slice(0, 160)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(409)
+    expect(failureText(result), `${why} (the same code, now with the guidance)`).toContain('INVALID_STATUS')
+    expect(failureText(result), why).toContain('114')
+    expect(snapshot(world), `${why} (a closed Case is never written to)`).toEqual(before)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it.each([
+    ['another customer', STRANGER, 404],
+    ['a worker', { id: STRANGER.id, role: 'worker' as const }, 403],
+    ['an admin', { id: CASE_ADMIN_ID, role: 'admin' as const }, 403],
+  ])('gives %s no guidance and no write for someone else\'s Case', async (_name, actor, status) => {
+    const world = activeWorld()
+    const before = snapshot(world)

+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(status)
+    expect(failureText(result), why).not.toContain('114')
+    expect(snapshot(world), why).toEqual(before)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+})

+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: a refusal never reveals more than the plain one to someone who does not own the Case`, () => {

+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: an invalid schedule does not hide an emergency`, () => {

+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: a closed Case refuses with the guidance and stays closed`, () => {
+  it.each(CLOSED)('turn on a %s Case', async (status) => {

+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(404)
+    expect(failureText(result), why).not.toContain('114')
+  })

+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: a refusal that carries the guidance is traced without the words that triggered it`, () => {
+  it('logs one redacted guidance-only trace for a closed Case, with no text and no actor pseudonym', async () => {
+    const world = activeWorld('hvac', { status: 'abandoned' })
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined)
+
+    const result = await world.request('', { message: GAS, language: 'vi' }, { identity: IDENTITY })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(409)
+    const logged = warn.mock.calls.filter((call) => call[0] === 'kael_turn_trace').map((call) => call[1] as Record<string, unknown>)
+    expect(logged.length, why).toBe(1)
+    expect(logged[0], why).toMatchObject({
+      route: 'case_emergency',
+      stage: 'emergency_gate',
+      outcome: 'emergency_guidance_only',
+      permission_reason: 'EMERGENCY_GAS',
+      gate_entry: 'turn',
+      correlation_id: expect.stringMatching(UUID),
+    })
+    const serialized = JSON.stringify(logged)
+    expect(serialized, `${why} (no customer text)`).not.toContain('gas ở bếp')
+    expect(serialized, why).not.toContain('actor_role_hash')
+    expect(serialized, why).not.toContain(OWNER.id)
+    warn.mockRestore()
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: a classified emergency whose write fails`, () => {
+  const traces = (warn: { mock: { calls: unknown[][] } }): Array<Record<string, unknown>> =>
+    warn.mock.calls.filter((call: unknown[]) => call[0] === 'kael_turn_trace').map((call: unknown[]) => call[1] as Record<string, unknown>)
+
+  it('still carries the guidance in the error, and leaves a redacted failure trace', async () => {
+    const world = pendingWorld('hvac')
+    armWriteFailures(world, { sessionUpdates: 2 })
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined)
+
+    const result = await world.request('', { message: GAS, language: 'vi' }, { identity: IDENTITY })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} body=${failureText(result).slice(0, 200)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(500)
+    expect(failureText(result), `${why} (the guidance is not lost with the write)`).toContain('114')
+    const logged = traces(warn)
+    expect(logged.length, why).toBe(1)
+    expect(logged[0], why).toMatchObject({
+      schema: 'kael_turn_trace.v1',
+      route: 'case_emergency',
+      stage: 'emergency_gate',
+      outcome: 'emergency_write_failed',
+      permission_reason: 'EMERGENCY_GAS',
+      gate_entry: 'turn',
+      correlation_id: expect.stringMatching(UUID),
+      edge_release_id: IDENTITY.releaseId,
+      edge_deployment_id: IDENTITY.deploymentId,
+      client_platform: 'ios',
+      client_build_number: 57,
+    })
+    expect(logged[0]?.emergency_gate_version, `${why} (the gate version is recorded)`).toEqual(expect.stringMatching(/^[a-z][a-z0-9_.]{2,47}$/))
+    const serialized = JSON.stringify(logged)
+    expect(serialized, `${why} (no customer text)`).not.toContain('gas ở bếp')
+    expect(serialized, `${why} (no actor pseudonym or identifier)`).not.toContain('actor_role_hash')
+    expect(serialized, why).not.toContain(OWNER.id)
+    warn.mockRestore()
+  })
+
+  it('carries the advice and a failure trace when the reply to advice-only wiring words cannot be written', async () => {
+    const WIRING = 'Ổ cắm tóe lửa khi cắm phích'
+    const reference = activeWorld('hvac')
+    const answered = await reference.request('', { message: WIRING, language: 'vi' })
+    expect(answered.status, pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'the reference request is answered')).toBe(200)
+    const advice = String(reference.kaelTurns().find((turn) => (turn.safe_metadata as Record<string, unknown>).emergency_route === 'electrical_water')?.text_content)
+    expect(advice.length, pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'the advice text exists')).toBeGreaterThan(20)
+
+    const world = activeWorld('hvac')
+    armWriteFailures(world, { replyInserts: 3 })
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined)
+    const result = await world.request('', { message: WIRING, language: 'vi' }, { identity: IDENTITY })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} body=${failureText(result).slice(0, 160)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(500)
+    expect(String(result.body.error), `${why} (the advice itself is not lost with the write)`).toContain(advice)
+    const logged = traces(warn)
+    expect(logged.map((trace) => trace.outcome), why).toEqual(['emergency_write_failed'])
+    expect(logged[0], why).toMatchObject({ permission_reason: 'EMERGENCY_ELECTRICAL_WATER', gate_entry: 'turn', correlation_id: expect.stringMatching(UUID) })
+    warn.mockRestore()
+  })
+
+  it('does not fabricate a success: no emergency reply is claimed and the Case is unmoved', async () => {
+    const world = pendingWorld('hvac')
+    armWriteFailures(world, { replyInserts: 2 })
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined)
+
+    const result = await world.request('', { message: GAS, language: 'vi' })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(500)
+    expect(routesOf(world), why).toEqual([])
+    expect((world.session().safe_metadata as Record<string, any>).intake_confirmation.status, why).toBe('pending')
+    warn.mockRestore()
+  })
+
+  it('leaves no failure trace when the emergency was answered, even after one retried write', async () => {
+    const world = pendingWorld('hvac')
+    armWriteFailures(world, { replyInserts: 1 })
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined)
+
+    const result = await world.request('', { message: GAS, language: 'vi' })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(traces(warn).map((trace) => trace.outcome), why).toEqual(['emergency_stop'])
+    warn.mockRestore()
+  })
+
+  it('does not trace or alter a failing write for an ordinary request', async () => {
+    const world = pendingWorld('hvac')
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined)
+
+    const result = await world.request('', { message: BENIGN, language: 'vi' })
+    expect(result.status, pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status}`)).toBe(409)
+    expect(traces(warn).filter((trace) => trace.route === 'case_emergency'), pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'no emergency trace')).toEqual([])
+    warn.mockRestore()
+  })
+})

+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
+
+import { pillarWhy, type PillarManifest } from '../../pillar-manifest'
+import { createMobileApiHandler } from '../../../../../../supabase/functions/mobile-api/_shared/http'
+import { buildInitialDiagnosisScopeArtifact, getKaelPerformanceProfile } from '../../../../../../supabase/functions/mobile-api/_shared/kael/index'
+import { buildKaelIntakeConfirmation } from '../../../../../../supabase/functions/mobile-api/_shared/kael/pipeline/intake-confirmation'
+import {
+  CASE_ADMIN_ID,
+  OWNER,
+  STRANGER,
+  assistantReply,
+  HVAC_UNIT_SLOT,
+  classifierReply,
+  createCaseWorld,
+  installEdgeRuntimeTestHooks,
+  unitAnswered,
+  type CaseWorld,
+  type ModelReplyFn,
+} from '../harness'
+
+export const PILLAR = {
+  id: 'P406-kael-case-emergency-intake',
+  invariant:
+    'a reported fire, medical, police or unclear emergency in a Case is answered by deterministic Vietnam emergency guidance before any model call, boundary decline or pipeline step, whether or not a playbook flag is on; the Case keeps its owner, facts, offer and phase, and no Job, quote, match or payment is created; ordinary service wording continues through the normal intake',
+  authority: [
+    'governance/RULES.md #7 (an emergency classification is not a workflow gate and never creates or advances money-impacting state)',
+    'governance/RULES.md #3 (the safety text is deterministic server copy, not raw model output)',
+    'governance/RULES.md #8 (a failed write is a visible error, never a fabricated success)',
+    'KAWA_01_Foundation_Safety_Evaluation.pdf W01 (Case intake emergency coverage, no flag bypass)',
+  ],
+  target: 'supabase/functions/mobile-api/_shared/domains/kael-chat/emergency-turn.ts',
+  layer: 'integration',
+  siblings: ['P401-kael-emergency-routing', 'P405-kael-emergency-signal-matrix', 'P398-kael-recurring-incident-e2e'],
+  mutation:
+    'make resolveCaseEmergency return null, or gate it behind the playbook flag: the flag-off rows reach the pipeline, a model is called and the guidance row turns red',
+} as const satisfies PillarManifest
+
+installEdgeRuntimeTestHooks()
+
+const SERVICES = ['electrical', 'plumbing', 'cleaning', 'hvac', 'upholstery', 'handyman'] as const
+type Service = typeof SERVICES[number]
+
+const healthyModel: ModelReplyFn = (call) => {
+  if (call.purpose === 'case_turn_intent') {
+    return classifierReply({ intent: 'provide_information', confidence: 0.9, pending_slot_answer: unitAnswered('1 máy') })
+  }
+  if (call.purpose === 'normal_chat_response') return assistantReply()
+  return { content: '{}' }
+}
+
+function worldFor(service: Service, options: Parameters<typeof createCaseWorld>[0] = {}) {
+  return createCaseWorld({
+    service,
+    artifact: buildInitialDiagnosisScopeArtifact({ serviceType: service, customerGoal: 'Cần hỗ trợ' }),
+    model: healthyModel,
+    ...options,
+  })
+}
+
+const clone = <T,>(value: T): T => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as T
+const emergencyTurns = (world: CaseWorld) =>
+  world.kaelTurns().filter((turn) => (turn.safe_metadata as Record<string, unknown>).emergency_route !== undefined)
+const caseIdentity = (world: CaseWorld) => {
+  const scope = world.scope()
+  return clone({ service_type: scope.service_type, facts: scope.facts, missing_facts: scope.missing_facts, evidence: scope.evidence })
+}
+
+const GENUINE: ReadonlyArray<readonly [Service, string, string, string]> = [
+  ['electrical', 'Ổ cắm đang bốc khói', 'fire', '114'],
+  ['plumbing', 'Ống nước vỡ và có người bị điện giật', 'medical', '115'],
+  ['cleaning', 'Có người đột nhập vào nhà', 'police', '113'],
+  ['hvac', 'Máy lạnh đang cháy', 'fire', '114'],
+  ['upholstery', 'Ghế sofa đang cháy', 'fire', '114'],
+  ['handyman', 'Thợ bị ngã bất tỉnh', 'medical', '115'],
+]
+
+const ORDINARY: ReadonlyArray<readonly [Service, string]> = [
+  ['electrical', 'Ổ cắm bị cháy từ tuần trước, nhờ thợ thay'],
+  ['plumbing', 'Nước đang chảy ra từ vòi'],
+  ['cleaning', 'Dọn nhà sau vụ hỏa hoạn tuần trước'],
+  ['hvac', 'Máy lạnh đang chạy nhưng không mát'],
+  ['upholstery', 'Sofa có vết cháy thuốc lá'],
+  ['handyman', 'Bếp gas có lửa yếu, nhờ thợ xem'],
+]
+
+describe.each([
+  ['playbook flags off', {} as Record<string, string>],
+  ['every playbook flag on', Object.fromEntries(SERVICES.map((service) => [`KAEL_PLAYBOOK_${service.toUpperCase()}_ENABLED`, 'true']))],
+])(`${PILLAR.id}: genuine emergencies stop the Case turn (%s)`, (_label, env) => {
+  it.each(GENUINE)('%s: "%s" -> %s guidance naming %s, with no model call and no business write', async (service, message, route, number) => {
+    // The flagged electrical path already carries its own critical guidance; it is covered below.
+    if (service === 'electrical' && Object.keys(env).length > 0) return
+    const world = worldFor(service, { env })
+    const identity = caseIdentity(world)
+
+    const result = await world.send(message)
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `${service}: status=${result.status} purposes=${result.purposes.join(',')} newTurns=${result.newTurns.length}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(result.calls, `${why} (a deterministic emergency reply asks no model)`).toEqual([])
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+    const reply = world.kaelTurns().at(-1)
+    expect(String(reply?.text_content), why).toContain(number)
+    expect(reply?.content_type, why).toBe('analysis')
+    expect(reply?.safe_metadata, why).toMatchObject({ emergency_route: route, safety_source: 'deterministic_emergency' })
+    expect(result.newTurns.map((turn) => turn.role), `${why} (the customer turn and one reply)`).toEqual(['customer', 'kael'])
+    expect(world.session().status, why).toBe('active')
+    expect(world.session().job_id, `${why} (an emergency never creates a Job)`).toBeNull()
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), `${why} (no quote, match or payment row)`).toEqual([])
+    expect(caseIdentity(world), `${why} (the Case keeps what the customer already told Kael)`).toEqual(identity)
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: the emergency log line carries no identity or text`, () => {
+  it('logs the route code only, never the session, the customer or the message', async () => {
+    const warn = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => {})
+    try {
+      const world = worldFor('hvac')
+      await world.send('Máy lạnh đang cháy, số điện thoại của tôi là 0901234567')
+
+      const logged = JSON.stringify(warn.mock.calls)
+      const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `log lines: ${logged.slice(0, 200)}`)
+      expect(logged, `${why} (the line is the shared turn trace, not a free-form message)`).toContain('kael_turn_trace')
+      expect(logged, why).toContain('"route":"case_emergency"')
+      expect(logged, why).toContain('"permission_reason":"EMERGENCY_FIRE"')
+      expect(logged, why).not.toContain('actor_role_hash')
+      expect(logged, why).not.toContain(OWNER.id)
+      expect(logged, why).not.toContain('39800000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001')
+      expect(logged, why).not.toContain('0901234567')
+      expect(logged, why).not.toContain('Máy lạnh')
+    } finally {
+      warn.mockRestore()
+    }
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: the flagged electrical path`, () => {
+  it('does not say it twice: critical guidance from the playbook path is the only safety message', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('electrical', { env: { KAEL_PLAYBOOK_ELECTRICAL_ENABLED: 'true' } })
+
+    const result = await world.send('Ổ cắm đang bốc khói')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} purposes=${result.purposes.join(',')}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world), `${why} (the gate defers to the structured path)`).toEqual([])
+    const guided = world.kaelTurns().filter((turn) => String(turn.text_content).includes('114'))
+    expect(guided.length, `${why} (exactly one reply carries the 114 guidance)`).toBe(1)
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('defers a smoke and sparking report to the HVAC flagged path instead of adding a second message', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('hvac', { env: { KAEL_PLAYBOOK_HVAC_ENABLED: 'true' } })
+
+    const result = await world.send('Máy lạnh nhà mình bốc khói và có mùi khét, tia lửa điện ở ổ cắm')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} purposes=${result.purposes.join(',')}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world), `${why} (the flagged path owns the hazard reply)`).toEqual([])
+    expect(result.purposes, `${why} (a safety turn is not routed through the classifier)`).not.toContain('deepseek:case_turn_intent')
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('still stops for a medical emergency, which the electrical playbook has no guidance for', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('electrical', { env: { KAEL_PLAYBOOK_ELECTRICAL_ENABLED: 'true' } })
+
+    const result = await world.send('Có người bị điện giật và bất tỉnh')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status}`)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+    expect(world.kaelTurns().at(-1)?.safe_metadata, why).toMatchObject({ emergency_route: 'medical' })
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: ordinary wording continues through the normal intake`, () => {
+  it.each(ORDINARY)('%s: "%s" is not treated as an emergency', async (service, message) => {
+    const world = worldFor(service)
+
+    const result = await world.send(message)
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `${service}: status=${result.status} purposes=${result.purposes.join(',')}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world), why).toEqual([])
+    expect(result.calls.length, `${why} (the normal intake still runs)`).toBeGreaterThan(0)
+  })
+
+  it('does not stop the Case for generic urgency alone', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('plumbing')
+
+    await world.send('Ống nước vỡ, cần hỗ trợ khẩn cấp')
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world), pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'urgent is a priority, not a reported danger')).toEqual([])
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: a wiring or water hazard advises, then the intake continues`, () => {
+  it('adds the advisory and still asks the Case its next question when no playbook flag is on', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('electrical')
+
+    const result = await world.send('Ổ cắm tóe lửa khi cắm phích')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} purposes=${result.purposes.join(',')}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    const advisory = emergencyTurns(world)
+    expect(advisory.map((turn) => (turn.safe_metadata as Record<string, unknown>).emergency_route), why).toEqual(['electrical_water'])
+    expect(String(advisory[0]?.text_content), why).toMatch(/114.*115.*112/)
+    expect(result.calls.length, `${why} (the intake was not abandoned)`).toBeGreaterThan(0)
+    expect(world.kaelTurns().length, `${why} (a reply follows the advisory)`).toBeGreaterThan(emergencyTurns(world).length)
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: the Case keeps its offer, its blocker and its phase`, () => {
+  it('leaves an offer under review in place after an emergency reply', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('hvac', { status: 'estimate_ready', casePhase: 'offer_review' })
+
+    const result = await world.send('Máy lạnh đang cháy')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} session.status=${String(world.session().status)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(world.session().status, why).toBe('estimate_ready')
+    expect(world.session().case_phase, why).toBe('offer_review')
+    expect(world.session().job_id, why).toBeNull()
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('puts back the recorded quote blocker the incoming turn cleared', async () => {
+    const world = createCaseWorld({ model: healthyModel })
+    await world.seedPriceEvidenceGap()
+    const before = clone({ blockers: world.scope().quote_blockers, next: world.scope().next_action })
+    expect(before.blockers.length, 'the fixture records a blocker').toBeGreaterThan(0)
+
+    await world.send('Máy lạnh đang cháy')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `blockers=${JSON.stringify(world.scope().quote_blockers)}`)
+    expect(world.scope().quote_blockers, why).toEqual(before.blockers)
+    expect(world.scope().next_action, why).toEqual(before.next)
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: the Case carries on after an emergency reply`, () => {
+  it('stores the next answer as a fact and does not repeat the emergency', async () => {
+    const world = createCaseWorld({ model: healthyModel })
+    await world.send('Máy lạnh đang cháy')
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world).length).toBe(1)
+
+    const next = await world.send('2 máy treo tường 1HP.')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${next.status} purposes=${next.purposes.join(',')} facts=${JSON.stringify(world.scope().facts)}`)
+    expect(next.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world).length, `${why} (an ordinary answer is not another emergency)`).toBe(1)
+    expect(world.scope().facts[HVAC_UNIT_SLOT], why).toContain('1 máy')
+    expect(world.session().job_id, why).toBeNull()
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('takes a correction that withdraws the danger as ordinary information', async () => {
+    const world = createCaseWorld({ model: healthyModel })
+    await world.send('Máy lạnh đang cháy')
+
+    const correction = await world.send('Xin lỗi, không có khói và không cháy, máy chỉ chạy ồn thôi')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${correction.status} purposes=${correction.purposes.join(',')}`)
+    expect(correction.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world).length, why).toBe(1)
+    expect(correction.calls.length, `${why} (the intake resumed)`).toBeGreaterThan(0)
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: refused actors, replays, the kill switch and a failed write`, () => {
+  it.each([
+    ['another customer', STRANGER, 404],
+    ['a worker', { id: STRANGER.id, role: 'worker' as const }, 403],
+    ['an admin', { id: CASE_ADMIN_ID, role: 'admin' as const }, 403],
+  ])('%s gets no emergency reply and writes nothing', async (_name, actor, status) => {
+    const world = worldFor('electrical')
+    const before = clone({ session: world.session(), turns: world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns })
+
+    const result = await world.send('Ổ cắm đang bốc khói', { as: actor })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} body=${JSON.stringify(result.body).slice(0, 120)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(status)
+    expect(clone({ session: world.session(), turns: world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns }), why).toEqual(before)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('answers a repeated report each time without creating any business record', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('hvac')
+
+    await world.send('Máy lạnh đang cháy')
+    await world.send('Máy lạnh đang cháy')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `kael turns=${world.kaelTurns().length}`)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world).length, why).toBe(2)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+    expect(world.session().job_id, why).toBeNull()
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('answers even when the AI kill switch is on', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('hvac', { env: { KAEL_AI_KILL_SWITCH: 'true' } })
+
+    const result = await world.send('Máy lạnh đang cháy')
+    expect(result.status, pillarWhy(PILLAR, 'kill switch')).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world).length).toBe(1)
+    expect(world.modelCalls).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('reports a failed reply write instead of pretending the guidance was delivered', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('hvac')
+    const client = world.db.client as unknown as { from: (table: string) => any }
+    const original = client.from.bind(client)
+    client.from = (table: string) => {
+      const query = original(table)
+      if (table !== 'kael_chat_turns') return query
+      const failing = {
+        select: () => failing,
+        single: () => failing,
+        then: (resolve: (value: unknown) => unknown) => Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: { code: 'XX000', message: 'write failed' } }).then(resolve),
+      }
+      return {
+        ...query,
+        insert: (value: Record<string, unknown>) => (value.role === 'kael' ? failing : query.insert(value)),
+      }
+    }
+
+    const result = await world.send('Máy lạnh đang cháy')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} body=${JSON.stringify(result.body).slice(0, 160)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(500)
+    expect(world.kaelTurns().filter((turn) => String(turn.text_content).includes('114')), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: confirming a booking intake whose description reports an emergency`, () => {
+  it('answers with guidance at the confirmation boundary and starts no analysis', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('hvac')
+    const profile = getKaelPerformanceProfile('hvac')
+    const date = new Date(Date.now() + 5 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10)
+    const scheduledAt = new Date(Date.UTC(Number(date.slice(0, 4)), Number(date.slice(5, 7)) - 1, Number(date.slice(8, 10)), 9 - 7, 0)).toISOString()
+    const confirmation = buildKaelIntakeConfirmation({
+      actorId: OWNER.id,
+      serviceType: 'hvac',
+      profileId: profile!.id,
+      description: 'Máy lạnh đang cháy trong phòng ngủ',
+      problemChips: ['other_hvac'],
+      addressLabel: 'Căn hộ thử nghiệm',
+      addressDistrict: 'Quận 7',
+      scheduledAt,
+      scheduleWindow: { date, start: '09:00', end: '11:00', time_zone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' },
+      language: 'vi',
+    } as never)
+    expect(confirmation.blocking, 'the fixture confirmation must be confirmable').toBe(false)
+    world.session().safe_metadata = { ...world.session().safe_metadata, intake_confirmation: confirmation }
+    const handler = createMobileApiHandler({
+      authenticate: async () => ({
+        success: true as const,
+        user: { id: OWNER.id },
+        role: OWNER.role,
+        supabase: world.db.client,
+        privilegedSupabase: world.db.client,
+        userSupabase: world.db.client,
+      }) as never,
+      services: world.services,
+    })
+
+    const response = await handler(new Request('https://edge.test/mobile-api/kael/chat/39800000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001/intake-confirmation', {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ decision: 'confirmed' }),
+    }))
+
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${response.status} kael=${world.kaelTurns().length}`)
+    expect(response.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, `${why} (no model for an emergency description)`).toEqual([])
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world).length, why).toBe(1)
+    expect(String(emergencyTurns(world)[0]?.text_content), why).toContain('114')
+    expect(world.session().job_id, why).toBeNull()
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: creating a Case with an emergency first message`, () => {
+  it('answers the first message with guidance and starts no analysis, quote or Job', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('electrical')
+    world.db.tables.kael_chat_sessions.length = 0
+    world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns.length = 0
+    // The Case-to-conversation link is unrelated to safety and uses PostgREST calls the in-memory stand-in lacks.
+    const inert: unknown = new Proxy(function () {}, {
+      get: (_target, property) => property === 'then'
+        ? (resolve: (value: unknown) => unknown) => Promise.resolve({ data: { id: 'conversation-stub' }, error: null }).then(resolve)
+        : () => inert,
+      apply: () => inert,
+    })
+    const client = world.db.client as unknown as { from: (table: string) => unknown }
+    const original = client.from.bind(client)
+    // Column defaults the real table applies on insert; the in-memory stand-in does not.
+    const sessionDefaults = {
+      job_id: null, status: 'active', case_phase: 'analysis', total_turns: 0, total_cost_usd: 0,
+      diagnosis_scope: null, started_at: '2026-10-20T00:00:00.000Z', created_at: '2026-10-20T00:00:00.000Z',
+      estimate_ready_at: null, scheduled_at: null, safe_metadata: {},
+    }
+    client.from = (table: string) => {
+      if (table === 'kael_customer_conversations') return inert
+      const query = original(table) as { insert: (value: Record<string, unknown>) => unknown }
+      if (table !== 'kael_chat_sessions') return query
+      const insert = query.insert.bind(query)
+      query.insert = (value) => insert({ ...sessionDefaults, ...value })
+      return query
+    }
+    const handler = createMobileApiHandler({
+      authenticate: async () => ({
+        success: true as const,
+        user: { id: OWNER.id },
+        role: OWNER.role,
+        supabase: world.db.client,
+        privilegedSupabase: world.db.client,
+        userSupabase: world.db.client,
+      }) as never,
+      services: world.services,
+    })
+
+    const response = await handler(new Request('https://edge.test/mobile-api/kael/chat', {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+      body: JSON.stringify({
+        service_type: 'electrical',
+        message: 'Ổ cắm đang bốc khói',
+        problem_chips: [],
+        photo_urls: [],
+        client_request_id: '40600000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',
+        language: 'vi',
+      }),
+    }))
+
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${response.status} turns=${world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns.length}`)
+    expect(response.status, why).toBeLessThan(300)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, `${why} (no model for an emergency first message)`).toEqual([])
+    const kael = world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns.filter((turn) => turn.role === 'kael')
+    expect(kael.length, why).toBe(1)
+    expect(String(kael[0]?.text_content), why).toContain('114')
+    expect(world.db.tables.kael_chat_sessions[0]?.job_id ?? null, why).toBeNull()
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: an emergency reported while submitting evidence`, () => {
+  async function submitEvidence(world: CaseWorld, body: Record<string, unknown>, actor = OWNER) {
+    const handler = createMobileApiHandler({
+      authenticate: async () => ({
+        success: true as const,
+        user: { id: actor.id },
+        role: actor.role,
+        supabase: world.db.client,
+        privilegedSupabase: world.db.client,
+        userSupabase: world.db.client,
+      }) as never,
+      services: world.services,
+    })
+    const response = await handler(new Request('https://edge.test/mobile-api/kael/chat/39800000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001/evidence', {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ language: 'vi', ...body }),
+    }))
+    return { status: response.status, body: await response.json() as Record<string, any> }
+  }
+
+  it.each(GENUINE)('%s: "%s" typed with a skipped evidence step -> %s guidance naming %s, with no flag and no model', async (service, message, route, number) => {
+    const world = worldFor(service)
+    const identity = caseIdentity(world)
+
+    const result = await submitEvidence(world, { decision: 'skipped', skip_reason: 'Không có ảnh', message })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `${service}: status=${result.status} body=${JSON.stringify(result.body).slice(0, 160)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, `${why} (a deterministic emergency reply asks no model)`).toEqual([])
+    const reply = world.kaelTurns().at(-1)
+    expect(String(reply?.text_content), why).toContain(number)
+    expect(reply?.safe_metadata, why).toMatchObject({ emergency_route: route, safety_source: 'deterministic_emergency' })
+    expect(world.session().job_id, why).toBeNull()
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+    expect(caseIdentity(world).service_type, why).toBe(identity.service_type)
+  })
+
+  it('answers an emergency spoken in a reviewed voice transcript', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('hvac')
+
+    const result = await submitEvidence(world, {
+      decision: 'confirmed',
+      evidence_items: [{ kind: 'voice_transcript', transcript: 'Máy lạnh đang cháy', model_eligible: true }],
+    })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} body=${JSON.stringify(result.body).slice(0, 160)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world).length, why).toBe(1)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('answers an emergency written in a reviewed text note', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('hvac')
+
+    const result = await submitEvidence(world, {
+      decision: 'confirmed',
+      evidence_items: [{ kind: 'text_note', transcript: 'Máy lạnh đang cháy', model_eligible: true }],
+    })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} body=${JSON.stringify(result.body).slice(0, 160)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world).length, why).toBe(1)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('continues the normal evidence analysis when the message is ordinary', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('hvac')
+
+    const result = await submitEvidence(world, { decision: 'skipped', skip_reason: 'Không có ảnh', message: 'Máy lạnh đang chạy nhưng không mát' })
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world), why).toEqual([])
+    expect(world.modelCalls.length, `${why} (the pipeline still ran)`).toBeGreaterThan(0)
+  })
+
+  it('refuses another customer without writing, and an emergency never creates a business record', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('hvac')
+    const before = clone({ session: world.session(), turns: world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns })
+
+    const refused = await submitEvidence(world, { decision: 'skipped', skip_reason: 'Không có ảnh', message: 'Máy lạnh đang cháy' }, STRANGER)
+    expect(refused.status, pillarWhy(PILLAR, JSON.stringify(refused.body).slice(0, 120))).toBe(404)
+    expect(clone({ session: world.session(), turns: world.db.tables.kael_chat_turns })).toEqual(before)
+
+    const answered = await submitEvidence(world, { decision: 'skipped', skip_reason: 'Không có ảnh', message: 'Máy lạnh đang cháy' })
+    expect(answered.status).toBe(200)
+    expect(world.session().job_id).toBeNull()
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites()).toEqual([])
+  })
+})
+
+describe(`${PILLAR.id}: fuel gas versus refrigerant in a Case`, () => {
+  const GAS_GENUINE: ReadonlyArray<readonly [Service, string]> = [
+    ['electrical', 'Đang sửa điện thì ngửi thấy mùi gas'],
+    ['plumbing', 'Bình gas bị rò rỉ ngay cạnh bồn rửa'],
+    ['cleaning', 'Lau bếp thì thấy bình gas bị rò'],
+    ['hvac', 'Máy lạnh chạy bình thường nhưng bếp có mùi gas'],
+    ['upholstery', 'Giặt sofa xong thấy mùi gas ở bếp'],
+    ['handyman', 'Bếp gas bị rò, nồng nặc mùi gas'],
+  ]
+
+  it.each(GAS_GENUINE)('%s: "%s" gets the gas guidance naming 114, not an out-of-scope decline, and asks no model', async (service, message) => {
+    const world = worldFor(service)
+
+    const result = await world.send(message)
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `${service}: status=${result.status} purposes=${result.purposes.join(',')}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+    expect(result.newTurns.map((turn) => turn.role), `${why} (what the customer said, then one reply)`).toEqual(['customer', 'kael'])
+    const reply = world.kaelTurns().at(-1)
+    expect(String(reply?.text_content), why).toContain('114')
+    expect(reply?.safe_metadata, why).toMatchObject({ emergency_route: 'gas', safety_source: 'deterministic_emergency' })
+    expect(world.session().status, why).toBe('active')
+    expect(world.session().job_id, why).toBeNull()
+    expect(world.unexpectedWrites(), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it.each([
+    ['hvac', 'Máy lạnh bị xì gas, cần nạp gas'],
+    ['hvac', 'Bị xì gas'],
+    ['hvac', 'Điều hòa thiếu gas, cần bơm gas R32'],
+    ['plumbing', 'Máy lạnh nhà mình rò gas, còn ống nước thì ổn'],
+    ['handyman', 'Bếp gas có lửa yếu, nhờ thợ xem'],
+    ['cleaning', 'Nhờ kiểm tra rò gas định kỳ cho bếp'],
+  ] as const)('%s: "%s" is an ordinary request and continues through the normal intake', async (service, message) => {
+    const world = worldFor(service)
+
+    const result = await world.send(message)
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `${service}: status=${result.status} purposes=${result.purposes.join(',')}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world), why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('asks for 112 about a bare "xì gas" outside the HVAC service, where it could be either gas', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('cleaning')
+
+    const result = await world.send('Bị xì gas')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world).map((turn) => (turn.safe_metadata as Record<string, unknown>).emergency_route), why).toEqual(['uncertain'])
+    expect(String(emergencyTurns(world)[0]?.text_content), why).toContain('112')
+    expect(world.modelCalls, why).toEqual([])
+  })
+
+  it('answers a gas leak even when the service would otherwise decline a gas stove as unsupported', async () => {
+    const world = worldFor('handyman')
+
+    const result = await world.send('Bếp gas bị rò, mùi gas nồng nặc')
+    const why = pillarWhy(PILLAR, `status=${result.status} body=${JSON.stringify(result.body).slice(0, 120)}`)
+    expect(result.status, why).toBe(200)
+    expect(emergencyTurns(world).length, why).toBe(1)
+    expect(world.session().status, `${why} (the Case is not abandoned or marked unsupported by the leak)`).toBe('active')
+  })
+})

+import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
+import { join, relative } from 'node:path'
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
+
+import { pillarWhy, type PillarManifest } from '../../pillar-manifest'
+import { createMobileApiHandler } from '../../../../../../supabase/functions/mobile-api/_shared/http'
+import { buildInitialDiagnosisScopeArtifact, getKaelPerformanceProfile } from '../../../../../../supabase/functions/mobile-api/_shared/kael/index'
+import { buildKaelIntakeConfirmation } from '../../../../../../supabase/functions/mobile-api/_shared/kael/pipeline/intake-confirmation'
+import {
+  CASE_ADMIN_ID,
+  CASE_SESSION_ID,
+  OWNER,

+  classifierReply,

+  installEdgeRuntimeTestHooks,
+  unitAnswered,
+  type Actor,
+  type CaseWorld,
+  type ModelReplyFn,
+} from '../harness'
